-<?php
-/*
- * Copyright (C) 2026 NHN COMMERCE. - All Rights Reserved
- *
- * Unauthorized copying or redistribution of this file in source and binary forms via any medium
- * is strictly prohibited.
- */
-
-namespace Bundle\Controller\Admin\Crm\Sms;
-
-use Origin\DTO\ApiClient\Commerce\Notification\MessageTemplateSmsDTO;
-use Origin\Enum\Crm\Message\SmsTemplateCategory;
-use Origin\Repository\Member\Sms\SmsContentsRepository;
-use Origin\Service\Crm\Message\SmsTemplateService;
-use Request;
-
-class SmsTemplatePsController extends \Controller\Admin\Controller
-{
-    public function index()
-    {
-        $request = Request::post()->toArray();
-        $mode = $request['mode'] ?? '';
-
-        $service = \App::getInstance(SmsTemplateService::class);
-
-        switch ($mode) {
-            case 'save':
-                $categoryValue = $request['category'] ?? '';
-                $category = SmsTemplateCategory::tryFrom($categoryValue);
-                if ($category === null) {
-                    $this->json(['success' => false, 'message' => '카테고리를 선택해주세요.']);
-                    return;
-                }
-
-                $subject = trim($request['subject'] ?? '');
-                if ($subject === '') {
-                    $this->json(['success' => false, 'message' => '제목을 입력해주세요.']);
-                    return;
-                }
-
-                $contents = trim($request['contents'] ?? '');
-                if ($contents === '') {
-                    $this->json(['success' => false, 'message' => '내용을 입력해주세요.']);
-                    return;
-                }
-
-                $templateDto = MessageTemplateSmsDTO::fromRegisterRequest([
-                    'templateCategory' => $category->value,
-                    'templateTitle' => $subject,
-                    'templateMessageContent' => $contents,
-                    'templateMessageTitle' => trim($request['messageTitle'] ?? ''),
-                ]);
-
-                $sno = $service->setSmsContents($templateDto);
-
-                if ($sno == 0) {
-                    $this->json(['success' => false, 'message' => '템플릿 저장에 실패하였습니다.']);
-                } else {
-                    $this->json(['success' => true, 'data' => ['sno' => $sno], 'message' => '템플릿이 저장되었습니다.']);
-                }
-
-                return;
-
-            default:
-                $this->json(['error' => 'Invalid mode', 'data' => []]);
-                return;
-        }
-    }
-}
```

+use Origin\Enum\Crm\Message\MessageSendMenuAccessVerdict;
+use Origin\Enum\Crm\Message\SmsSendRestrictType;
+use Origin\Service\Crm\Message\MessageSendMenuAccessService;
 
 class LayerSmsPasswordPsController extends \Controller\Admin\Controller
 {
@@ -26,6 +29,11 @@ class LayerSmsPasswordPsController extends \Controller\Admin\Controller
         $request = \App::getInstance('request');
         $isChangePassword = $request->post()->get('mode', 'changePassword') === 'changePassword';
         if ($isChangePassword) {
+            $restrictMessage = $this->getSmsRestrictMessage();
+            if ($restrictMessage !== null) {
+                $this->json(['error' => 1, 'message' => $restrictMessage]);
+            }
+
             $captchaNumber = $request->post()->get('captcha');
             $captcha = new Captcha();
             $result = $captcha->verify($captchaNumber, 1);
@@ -75,4 +83,31 @@ class LayerSmsPasswordPsController extends \Controller\Admin\Controller
             );
         }
     }
+
+
+    /**
+     * SMS 인증번호 변경은 SMS 관리자만 가능하다.
+     * ps 는 인터셉터 게이트를 타지 않아 화면 제한을 우회한 직접 호출이 가능하므로 조작 시점에 다시 본다.
+     * 판정축은 진입 게이트와 동일(역할·차단·IP·진입 점유인증). 책임자 미연동은 등록 안내 플로우라 차단하지 않는다.
+     */
+    protected function getSmsRestrictMessage(): ?string
+    {
+        /** @var MessageSendMenuAccessService $menuAccessService */
+        $menuAccessService = \App::getInstance(MessageSendMenuAccessService::class);
+        $verdict = $menuAccessService->evaluateEntry((int)\App::getInstance('session')->get('manager.sno'));
+
+        $writableVerdicts = [
+            null,
+            MessageSendMenuAccessVerdict::NOT_CONNECTED_REPRESENTATIVE,
+            MessageSendMenuAccessVerdict::NOT_CONNECTED_MANAGER,
+        ];
+
+        if (in_array($verdict, $writableVerdicts, true)) {
+            return null;
+        }
+
+        $restrictType = SmsSendRestrictType::fromVerdict($verdict);
+
+        return $restrictType->getClickAlertMessage() . ' ' . $restrictType->getClickAlertSubMessage();
+    }
 }
```
